# Genesis: picture prompts

370 pictures, one for each scene, covering every verse of Genesis. For each: the name to save
the picture as (or use Add picture on the app's Pictures page, which names it for you), its verses,
and the prompt to paste into ChatGPT. Every prompt ends with the same style and framing, so the
pictures look alike: warm and cinematic, tall (9:16), wide, with the people in the lower half.

## Chapter 1

### 1:1–2 · In the beginning

Save as: `Genesis 1.1-2`

> Seen from just above the water, a vast dark ocean stretches to every horizon beneath a black, starless sky, formless and empty. Low over the face of the deep, a faint shimmering radiance moves like a breath of wind, stirring the still water into long silver ripples, the first quiet movement in a silent, waiting world. God is never shown as a person, only as radiant light. Style: cinematic and photorealistic with a warm, painterly glow, like a still from a modern Bible film: rich warm colours, soft haze and gentle backlight, highly detailed faces and textured fabrics; golden-hour light unless the scene says otherwise. Historically accurate ancient Near Eastern clothing, tents and buildings. Vertical 9:16. A wide shot, the camera pulled back so the whole scene and its setting show. Everyone's heads in the lower half of the picture; the top half open (sky, hills, sea or buildings). Main action centred. No text, letters or numbers anywhere.
